<!-- GOJO -->
<section class="char-section" id="gojo">
<div class="char-header">
<h2 class="char-name">Gojo Satoru</h2>
<div class="char-title">The Honored One</div>
<div class="char-meta"><span>Zoner / Combo Technician</span><span>S-Tier Flagship</span></div>
<p class="tagline">Precision teleport combos > Earned 60s unblockable power window > Tactical domain/nuke decisions.</p>
</div>
<p class="description">Gojo embodies controlled omnipotence. Base kit rewards precise teleport combos managing Ult bar as resource. Six Eyes transforms him into unblockable force for 60 seconds. Mastery lies in choosing WHEN to deploy Domain, Hollow Nuke, and Red Boomerang within that window.</p>
<div class="section-label">3-Bar Integration</div>
<div class="bars-grid">
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">Ult Bar</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li><strong>Fill:</strong> Slow passive. Bonus on BF and R-combos.</li><li><strong>Cost:</strong> R costs 5-10%. Recoups 3-7% on hit. Net 2-3%.</li><li><strong>Activation:</strong> G at full > 2s cutscene+i-frames > 60s timer.</li><li><strong>Timer:</strong> Pauses if stunned. Nuke consumes ALL remaining.</li><li><strong>Post:</strong> Reset 0%. 15s burnout (+5% R cost).</li></ul></div>
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">Evasive Bar</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li><strong>Gain:</strong> Standard on damage/block.</li><li><strong>Base:</strong> Cancels BASE Blue pull.</li><li><strong>Six Eyes:</strong> USELESS vs Max Blue suction.</li><li><strong>Domain:</strong> Disabled for opponents inside.</li></ul></div>
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">R Special</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li><strong>CD:</strong> 8s base.</li><li><strong>SFX:</strong> Glass break on activation.</li><li><strong>Base:</strong> 5-10% Ult cost. Recoups on combo hit.</li><li><strong>Six Eyes:</strong> Free instant TP. Chant nuke. 0.2 Feint.</li><li><strong>Burnout:</strong> +5% cost for 15s post-ult.</li></ul></div>
</div>
<div class="section-label">Base Moves</div>
<table class="moves-table"><thead><tr><th class="input-col">In</th><th class="name-col">Name</th><th>Function</th><th class="block-col">Block</th><th class="notes-col">Details</th></tr></thead><tbody>
<tr><td class="input-col">1</td><td class="name-col">Lapse Blue</td><td>Pull + upward kick</td><td class="block-col">Yes</td><td class="notes-col">Evasive cancels pull. 12 stud max. 12f startup. 18f whiff. Blue gravitational VFX.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">2</td><td class="name-col">Reversal Red</td><td>Slow orb, 15-stud explode</td><td class="block-col">Partial</td><td class="notes-col">10-15% through block. 6 stud radius. 8f startup. Safe at range.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">3</td><td class="name-col">Rapid Punches</td><td>Multi-hit barrage</td><td class="block-col">No</td><td class="notes-col">6-8 studs. NO tracking. 6 hits. Last hit endlag if blocked. 22f whiff.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">4</td><td class="name-col">Twofold Kick</td><td>2-hit kick combo</td><td class="block-col">No</td><td class="notes-col">Second launches ~15 studs at 45deg. 14f startup. 24f whiff.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">R</td><td class="name-col">Teleport</td><td>Glass break SFX teleport</td><td class="block-col">N/A</td><td class="notes-col">5-10% Ult cost. Recoups 3-7% on combo hit. Universal audio tell.</td></tr>
</tbody></table>
<div class="section-label">R Combos</div>
<table class="moves-table"><thead><tr><th class="input-col">In</th><th class="name-col">Variant</th><th>Sequence</th><th class="notes-col">Net Cost / Notes</th></tr></thead><tbody>
<tr><td class="input-col">1+R</td><td class="name-col">Blue TP Slam</td><td>Blue>TP Up>Downslam</td><td class="notes-col">-2% net. High commitment. 30f whiff recovery.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">2+R</td><td class="name-col">Red Point Blank</td><td>Red>TP Behind>Close Red</td><td class="notes-col">-2% net. 5-8f shimmer telegraph before TP.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">3+R</td><td class="name-col">Face Drag</td><td>Barrage End>TP>Drag</td><td class="notes-col">-2% net. Combo extender ONLY. Cannot use raw.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">1+2+R</td><td class="name-col">Aerial Red Trap</td><td>Kick>TP Behind Air>Red</td><td class="notes-col">-2% net. High-skill aerial tech.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">4+2+R</td><td class="name-col">Levitate Red</td><td>Kick>Levitate 1s>Red</td><td class="notes-col">-3% net. Enemy stunned. Highest risk/reward.</td></tr>
</tbody></table>
<div class="section-label">Ultimate</div>
<div class="ult-box">
<div class="ult-name">Six Eyes</div>
<div class="ult-voiceline">I'm surprised you thought you could win against me with a pathetic excuse for a brain like that.</div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Activation</div><div class="ult-detail-text">G at full bar. 2s blindfold removal + full i-frames. 60s countdown timer. Pauses if stunned. Does NOT pause during Domain.</div></div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Six Eyes State</div><ul class="ult-detail-text"><li><strong>All moves UNBLOCKABLE.</strong></li><li><strong>Max Blue (1):</strong> Giant movable orb. INESCAPABLE suction. 12-stud max. Scales with distance. 45 dmg. Persists 3s after kill.</li><li><strong>Max Red (2):</strong> Large fast projectile. Visible trail. Linear. ~0.8s travel.</li><li><strong>Hollow Purple (3):</strong> Full i-frames during 1.5s charge. 20s internal CD. 0.5s vulnerability after release.</li><li><strong>Domain (4):</strong> 14s. Consumes 14s timer. ALL enemies FULLY STUNNED. 1.5x damage. Free movement. Ends early if >30% HP taken.</li></ul></div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Hollow Nuke (1+2+R)</div><ul class="ult-detail-text"><li><strong>Base:</strong> Kill with Blue>Shoot Red into orb. Massive AoE. Consumes ALL timer.</li><li><strong>Chanted (Hold R):</strong> +50% dmg, +30% radius. 1.5s chant. 25% self-dmg. IMMOBILE. I-frames OFF. Interruptible.</li></ul></div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Red Boomerang (2+R)</div><div class="ult-detail-text">Works base AND Six Eyes. Returns on hit. Camera steer (smooth). Return hit launches enemy for Blockflash. Miss = dissipates. 70% return speed.</div></div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">0.2 Domain Feint (G+R)</div><ul class="ult-detail-text"><li><strong>Stage 1:</strong> 0.3s. Cancel anytime. Bait evasive.</li><li><strong>Stage 2:</strong> 0.5s. Cancel first 0.3s.</li><li><strong>Stage 3:</strong> 0.7s. Cancel first 0.4s. Brief stun if completed.</li><li>Full i-frames all stages. Cancel preserves timer.</li></ul></div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Post-Ult</div><div class="ult-detail-text">Reset 0%. 15s burnout (+5% R cost). 0.5s recovery animation.</div></div>
</div>
<div class="section-label">Balance Safeguards</div>
<ul class="safeguards-list">
<li>R-combos net 2-3% Ult cost. Rewards execution.</li>
<li>Teleports have 5-8f shimmer telegraph.</li>
<li>Mini Red partial block = 10-15% only.</li>
<li>Rapid Punches no tracking + punishable endlag.</li>
<li>Max Blue suction scales with distance.</li>
<li>Hollow Purple 20s internal CD. Max 2-3 per Six Eyes.</li>
<li>Domain ends early at >30% HP taken.</li>
<li>Nuke chant = 25% self-dmg + immobile + interruptible.</li>
<li>Boomerang requires manual camera steering.</li>
<li>0.2 Feint preserves timer on cancel.</li>
<li>Post-ult burnout ensures consequence.</li>
</ul>
<div class="section-label">Counterplay</div>
<div class="counterplay-grid">
<div class="counterplay-cell"><div class="counterplay-phase">Pre-Activation</div><ul class="counterplay-methods"><li>Punish 0.5s post-cutscene vulnerability</li><li>Force bad timing activation</li><li>Read Gojo player patterns</li></ul></div>
<div class="counterplay-cell"><div class="counterplay-phase">During Six Eyes</div><ul class="counterplay-methods"><li>Stay outside 12-stud Blue range</li><li>Kite/stall for 60s</li><li>Count Purple CD (20s downtime)</li><li>Focus fire for Domain early end</li></ul></div>
<div class="counterplay-cell"><div class="counterplay-phase">Post-Ult</div><ul class="counterplay-methods"><li>Punish 15s burnout (+5% R cost)</li><li>Capitalize on 0.5s recovery</li><li>Bait panicked nuke chant</li></ul></div>
</div>
</section>

<!-- YUJI / SUKUNA -->
<section class="char-section" id="yuji">
<div class="char-header">
<h2 class="char-name">Yuji Itadori / Sukuna</h2>
<div class="char-title">The Vessel & The King of Curses</div>
<div class="char-meta"><span>Rushdown Brawler / Dual-Form</span><span>A-Tier Core</span></div>
<p class="tagline">Timing-based combos > Feint mixups > Earned Sukuna awakening.</p>
</div>
<p class="description">Base Yuji rewards precise Divergent Fist timing and feint tech. Sukuna awakening earned through skilled play transforms him into unblockable catastrophe. Duality tells story: determined brawler earns darkness through execution.</p>
<div class="section-label">3-Bar Integration</div>
<div class="bars-grid">
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">Ult Bar</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li><strong>Fill:</strong> Medium. Bonus on Black Flash chains.</li><li><strong>R Cost:</strong> 6% per feint. Scales consecutive (6/8/10%). Resets on hit.</li><li><strong>Activation:</strong> G>Enchain mouth+2s cutscene>Sukuna 60s.</li><li><strong>Post:</strong> Yuji returns. Reset 0%. 15s burnout.</li></ul></div>
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">Evasive Bar</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li><strong>Gain:</strong> Standard on damage.</li><li><strong>Utility:</strong> Cancels mistimed air slams.</li><li><strong>Critical:</strong> Escape Sukuna unblockables.</li></ul></div>
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">R Special</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li><strong>CD:</strong> NONE. Instant.</li><li><strong>Cost:</strong> 6% Ult (scaling consecutive).</li><li><strong>Recovery:</strong> 5f (8f same-move consecutive).</li><li><strong>No Feint:</strong> Move 4 (Manji Kick).</li><li><strong>Works On:</strong> Ground 1/2/3 + Air+1/Air+2.</li></ul></div>
</div>
<div class="section-label">Base Moves (Yuji)</div>
<table class="moves-table"><thead><tr><th class="input-col">In</th><th class="name-col">Name</th><th>Function</th><th class="block-col">Block</th><th class="notes-col">Details</th></tr></thead><tbody>
<tr><td class="input-col">1</td><td class="name-col">Cursed Strikes</td><td>Multi-punch, red eye trail</td><td class="block-col">Gnd:Yes Air:No</td><td class="notes-col">Ground: 8 studs, 10f, safe poke. Air: Unblockable slam, 10 stud cone, 12f, 10f landing lag. Both feintable.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">2</td><td class="name-col">Crushing Blow</td><td>Two CE slams</td><td class="block-col">No</td><td class="notes-col">Always unblockable. 7 studs. Second launches ~8 studs. 14f startup. 22f whiff.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">3</td><td class="name-col">Divergent Fist</td><td>Timing-based</td><td class="block-col">No</td><td class="notes-col"><strong>Single:</strong> CE punch 12f. <strong>Double mistimed (8-10f):</strong> 2s stun. <strong>Double perfect (3-5f):</strong> Black Flash. <strong>Back-hit (90deg):</strong> No CD, chain x4 (+10% each). 4th ALWAYS CDs. 6 studs.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">4</td><td class="name-col">Manji Kick</td><td>Counter projectiles/melee</td><td class="block-col">N/A</td><td class="notes-col">CANNOT feint. 15-20f active. Not grabs/domains. Whiff=25f. Success=launch ~12 studs.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">R</td><td class="name-col">Feint</td><td>Cancel move to neutral</td><td class="block-col">N/A</td><td class="notes-col">Instant/No CD. 6% Ult scaling. 5f recovery. Red flash+whoosh tell.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">Air+1</td><td class="name-col">Downward Slam</td><td>Feet-first drop</td><td class="block-col">No</td><td class="notes-col">Feintable. 10f landing lag.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">Air+2</td><td class="name-col">Air Dash Slams</td><td>1s hover>two slams</td><td class="block-col">No</td><td class="notes-col">Feintable. 15f landing lag.</td></tr>
</tbody></table>
<div class="section-label">Ultimate: Sukuna Awakening</div>
<div class="ult-box">
<div class="ult-name">Sukuna Awakening</div>
<div class="ult-voiceline">Enchain.</div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Activation</div><div class="ult-detail-text">G at full. Enchain mouth on face. 2s cutscene+i-frames. Sukuna 60s. Pauses if stunned. Post: Yuji returns, reset 0%, 15s burnout (+5% R).</div></div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Sukuna Moves</div><ul class="ult-detail-text"><li><strong>Dismantle (1):</strong> Multi-slash. UNBLOCKABLE. 12 studs. No tracking. 8f startup. 16f whiff.</li><li><strong>Fuga (2):</strong> Fire arrow. UNBLOCKABLE. +30% in Shrine. Startup 1.5s: no i-frames, cancelable, self-dmg zone 8 studs 5DPS, last 0.3s armor. Active 0.8s: full i-frames. Recovery 0.5s vulnerable.</li><li><strong>Rush (3):</strong> Dash>uppercut>TP>downslam. UNBLOCKABLE. 0.2s telegraph. Uppercut whiff=25f. Downslam lag=15f.</li><li><strong>Shrine (4):</strong> 12s domain. Block -50%. Dash-out first 0.5s only. Trapped after. Ends early >30% HP.</li><li><strong>Cleave (R):</strong> Neck grab+cuts. UNBLOCKABLE. 6 studs. 8% timer cost. 20f whiff.</li><li><strong>Air WCS (Air+1):</strong> ~40% ground WCS dmg. Safe on block.</li></ul></div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">World Cutting Slash (1>3>2>R)</div><ul class="ult-detail-text"><li>0.3s between inputs. Move 1 must be off-CD. 2/3 can be on CD (bypassed).</li><li>Ignores block. Massive true damage. Full i-frames 0.8s.</li><li>Costs 15% timer. Blade shimmer+slicing SFX tell.</li></ul></div>
</div>
<div class="section-label">Balance Safeguards</div>
<ul class="safeguards-list">
<li>R-feint 6% Ult scaling. Prevents spam.</li>
<li>Feint 5f recovery (8f consecutive). Punishable.</li>
<li>BF window 3-5f. Back-hit 90deg cone. Chain cap 4.</li>
<li>Manji Kick 15-20f active. Whiff 25f. No grab/domain counter.</li>
<li>Air landing lag 10f/15f. Feint removes but costs Ult.</li>
<li>Dismantle no tracking + 12 stud cap.</li>
<li>Fuga startup interruptible. Self-dmg zone 8 studs.</li>
<li>Rush uppercut whiff 25f. 0.2s telegraph.</li>
<li>Shrine dash-out 0.5s only.</li>
<li>Cleave 6 studs + 8% timer + 20f whiff.</li>
<li>WCS exact sequence + Move 1 off-CD + 15% timer.</li>
<li>Post-awakening 15s burnout +5% R.</li>
</ul>
<div class="section-label">Counterplay</div>
<div class="counterplay-grid">
<div class="counterplay-cell"><div class="counterplay-phase">Base Yuji</div><ul class="counterplay-methods"><li>Block Ground 1 safely</li><li>Space outside DF range (6 studs)</li><li>Turn to prevent back-hit BF</li><li>Punish Manji whiff (25f)</li><li>Read feint tells</li><li>Anti-air Air+1/Air+2</li></ul></div>
<div class="counterplay-cell"><div class="counterplay-phase">Sukuna</div><ul class="counterplay-methods"><li>Space outside Dismantle (12 studs)</li><li>Interrupt Fuga startup (first 1.2s)</li><li>Evasive through Fuga active (0.4s)</li><li>Dash out Shrine (0.5s)</li><li>Punish Rush uppercut whiff (25f)</li><li>Space outside Cleave (6 studs)</li><li>Avoid WCS positioning</li><li>Survive 60s > punish burnout</li></ul></div>
</div>
</section>

<!-- HAKARI -->
<section class="char-section" id="hakari">
<div class="char-header">
<h2 class="char-name">Hakari Kinji</h2>
<div class="char-title">The Restless Gambler</div>
<div class="char-meta"><span>Setup Trapper / Attrition Immortal</span><span>A-Tier Core</span></div>
<p class="tagline">Setup traps > Build Jackpot Meter > 4m11s attrition immortality.</p>
</div>
<p class="description">Base kit uses Reserve Balls and Shutter Doors to control arena and build Jackpot Meter. Jackpot grants 4m11s immortality with reduced damage, forcing attrition. Void throws provide free kills. The Dance reduces cooldowns for sustained pressure.</p>
<div class="section-label">3-Bar Integration</div>
<div class="bars-grid">
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">Ult (Jackpot)</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li><strong>Fill:</strong> M1 +2%. Move 3/4 +5%. Counter +10%. Ball+Door +20%. NO passive.</li><li><strong>Activation:</strong> 100% > G > Domain.</li><li><strong>Jackpot:</strong> 4m11s. Auto-heal 2.5%/s. Unlimited CE. -50% damage. Cap ~600 HP.</li></ul></div>

-   **Subtitle Display:** Currently selected technique name always visible in HUD subtitle text.
-   **Loss Condition:** All copies lost on death. Respawns with empty wheel.

### ⚖️ WHY DOMAIN BREAKING IS GENIUS BALANCE

| Aspect | Design Purpose |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Massive Cost** | You sacrifice your entire remaining domain duration (could be 60+ seconds) for ONE devastating strike. True gamble. |
| **No Spam** | Domain must be re-earned through ult bar fill. Cannot chain Ladders. Each use is a monumental decision. |
| **Target Commitment** | Once aimed and confirmed, you're locked into the cinematic. No canceling mid-sequence. |
| **Opponent Agency** | They see the domain shatter + sky rings form. They KNOW what's coming but can't escape (stunned). Psychological horror. |
| **Visual Storytelling** | Black silhouette ascending = literal depiction of soul being judged/purified. Matches JJK themes perfectly. |
| **Clip-Worthy Moment** | Every Jacob's Ladder will be screenshot/recorded. Free marketing for *Cursed Fate*. |

| **On Fail** | Full 4s cooldown applies. Marker gone. Must wait. |

### ⚖️ CRITICAL BALANCE SAFEGUARDS
1.  **Ratio Invisible:** Opponents can't react to marker. Must predict Nanami's timing. Pure mind game.
2.  **10s Timer:** Forces aggression. Can't camp with ratio active.
3.  **Circle Speed Scales:** Low HP = faster circle = harder to time. Rewards finishing wounded opponents quickly.
4.  **R CD Reset Condition:** Must land ratio AND follow-up move. Prevents free resets.
5.  **Limb Dislocation Randomized:** Not player-chosen. Prevents targeted shutdown.
6.  **Sharpen Limb Cut Permanent:** Only during Overtime. Resets post-ult. High stakes.
7.  **Interrogate Chase Fail State:** Too far = sequence cancels. Rewards positioning/wall throws.
8.  **Collapse Auto-Timer:** 6s fallback prevents infinite debris camping.
9.  **Ratio Breaker 4/4 Gate:** Requires 3 prior ratio confirms. Miss any = restart. 500 dmg earned, not given.
10. **Overtime R No CD:** Balanced by 2m timer + post-ult burnout.

| **Good Lock** | Circle within 6-stud distance → Ratio PARTIALLY locked. Marker stays for **5 seconds**. Reduced damage bonus. |
| **Miss** | Circle too far from red line → Ratio FAILS. Marker disappears immediately. Full 4s cooldown applies. |
| **No Second Press** | If 10 seconds pass without second R press → Circle expires. Marker disappears. Full 4s cooldown. |

<!-- GOJO -->
<section class="char-section" id="gojo">
<div class="char-header"><h2 class="char-name">Gojo Satoru</h2><div class="char-title">The Honored One</div><div class="char-meta"><span>Zoner / Combo Technician</span><span>S-Tier</span></div><p class="tagline">Precision teleport combos > Earned 60s unblockable window > Tactical domain/nuke decisions.</p></div>
<p class="description">Controlled omnipotence. Base kit = teleport combos managing Ult as resource. Six Eyes = 60s unblockable force. Mastery = WHEN to deploy Domain, Nuke, Boomerang.</p>
<div class="section-label">3-Bar Integration</div>
<div class="bars-grid">
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">Ult</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li><strong>Fill:</strong> Slow. Bonus on BF/R-combos.</li><li><strong>Cost:</strong> R 5-10%, recoup 3-7% on hit. Net 2-3%.</li><li><strong>Activate:</strong> G>2s cutscene+i-frames>60s timer. Pauses if stunned.</li><li><strong>Post:</strong> Reset 0. 15s burnout +5% R.</li></ul></div>
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">Evasive</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li>Standard gain.</li><li>Cancels BASE Blue pull.</li><li>USELESS vs Six Eyes Max Blue.</li><li>Disabled inside Unlimited Void.</li></ul></div>
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">R</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li>8s CD. Glass break SFX.</li><li>Base 5-10% Ult cost.</li><li>Six Eyes: free TP, chant, 0.2 feint.</li><li>Burnout +5% for 15s.</li></ul></div>
</div>
<div class="section-label">Base Moves</div>
<table class="moves-table"><thead><tr><th class="input-col">In</th><th class="name-col">Name</th><th>Function</th><th class="block-col">Block</th><th class="notes-col">Details / VFX / SFX</th></tr></thead><tbody>
<tr><td class="input-col">1</td><td class="name-col">Lapse Blue</td><td>Pull + up kick</td><td class="block-col">Yes</td><td class="notes-col">12 stud max. 12f startup. 18f whiff. VFX: blue gravitational distortion. SFX: low hum > suction crack. Evasive cancels pull.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">2</td><td class="name-col">Reversal Red</td><td>Slow orb 15-stud explode</td><td class="block-col">Partial</td><td class="notes-col">10-15% through block. 6 stud radius. 8f startup. VFX: crimson orb + radial burst. SFX: high charge > muffled boom.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">3</td><td class="name-col">Rapid Punches</td><td>6-hit barrage</td><td class="block-col">No</td><td class="notes-col">6-8 studs. NO tracking. Last hit endlag if blocked. 22f whiff. VFX: blue blur trails. SFX: rapid impacts + distinct final.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">4</td><td class="name-col">Twofold Kick</td><td>2-hit kick, launch</td><td class="block-col">No</td><td class="notes-col">Launches ~15 studs 45deg. 14f startup. 24f whiff. VFX: blue arcs + upward burst. SFX: heavy kicks + whoosh.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">R</td><td class="name-col">Teleport</td><td>Instant reposition</td><td class="block-col">N/A</td><td class="notes-col">5-10% Ult. Recoup on combo. SFX: glass break (universal tell).</td></tr>
</tbody></table>
<div class="section-label">R Combos</div>
<table class="moves-table"><thead><tr><th class="input-col">In</th><th class="name-col">Name</th><th>Sequence</th><th class="notes-col">Cost/Notes</th></tr></thead><tbody>
<tr><td class="input-col">1+R</td><td class="name-col">Blue TP Slam</td><td>Blue>TP Up>Downslam</td><td class="notes-col">-2% net. 30f whiff recovery.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">2+R</td><td class="name-col">Red Point Blank</td><td>Red>TP Behind>Close Red</td><td class="notes-col">-2% net. 5-8f shimmer telegraph.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">3+R</td><td class="name-col">Face Drag</td><td>Barrage>TP>Drag</td><td class="notes-col">-2% net. Extender only, no raw.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">1+2+R</td><td class="name-col">Aerial Red Trap</td><td>Kick>TP Behind Air>Red</td><td class="notes-col">-2% net. Reads jump timing.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">4+2+R</td><td class="name-col">Levitate Red</td><td>Kick>Levitate 1s>Red</td><td class="notes-col">-3% net. Enemy stunned. Highest risk.</td></tr>
</tbody></table>
<div class="section-label">Ultimate</div>
<div class="ult-box">
<div class="ult-name">Six Eyes</div>
<div class="ult-voiceline">I'm surprised you thought you could win against me with a pathetic excuse for a brain like that.</div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Activation</div><div class="ult-detail-text">G at full. 2s blindfold removal + full i-frames. 60s countdown. Pauses if stunned. VFX: white glow aura. SFX: rising hum.</div></div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Six Eyes Moves</div><ul class="ult-detail-text"><li><strong>All UNBLOCKABLE.</strong></li><li><strong>Max Blue (1):</strong> Movable orb. INESCAPABLE suction. 12 stud max. 45 dmg. Persists 3s after kill. VFX: swirling vortex + screen distortion. SFX: gravitational hum.</li><li><strong>Max Red (2):</strong> Fast projectile, visible trail, linear, ~0.8s travel. VFX: red comet. SFX: roar > detonation.</li><li><strong>Hollow Purple (3):</strong> i-frames during 1.5s charge. 20s internal CD. 0.5s vulnerable after. VFX: purple/black sphere > beam. SFX: ominous charge > reality tear.</li><li><strong>Unlimited Void (4):</strong> 14s. Consumes 14s timer. ALL enemies FULLY STUNNED. 1.5x dmg. Free movement. Ends early >30% HP. VFX: purple hue + cosmic backdrop. SFX: ambient hum.</li></ul></div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Hollow Nuke (1+2+R)</div><ul class="ult-detail-text"><li>Kill with Blue > Red into orb > Hold R chant.</li><li><strong>Base:</strong> Massive AoE. Consumes ALL timer.</li><li><strong>Chanted:</strong> +50% dmg +30% radius. 1.5s chant. 25% self-dmg. IMMOBILE. Interruptible. VFX: fusion glow > white explosion. SFX: chant > cataclysm.</li></ul></div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Red Boomerang (2+R)</div><div class="ult-detail-text">Returns on hit. Camera steer. Return hit launches for Blockflash. Miss = dissipate. 70% return speed. VFX: red arc. SFX: boomerang whoosh.</div></div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">0.2 Domain Feint (G+R)</div><ul class="ult-detail-text"><li>Stage 1 0.3s cancel anytime. Stage 2 0.5s cancel 0.3s. Stage 3 0.7s cancel 0.4s (brief stun if done).</li><li>Full i-frames. Cancel preserves timer. VFX: flickering boundary. SFX: teased activation + cancel chirp.</li></ul></div>
</div>
<div class="section-label">Balance Safeguards</div>
<ul class="safeguards-list"><li>R-combos net 2-3% cost.</li><li>TP shimmer 5-8f telegraph.</li><li>Mini Red partial 10-15%.</li><li>Punches no tracking + endlag.</li><li>Blue suction scales w/ distance.</li><li>Purple 20s CD max 2-3.</li><li>Domain early end >30% HP.</li><li>Nuke chant self-dmg + immobile.</li><li>Boomerang manual steer.</li><li>Feint preserves timer.</li><li>Burnout 15s +5% R.</li></ul>
<div class="section-label">Counterplay</div>
<div class="counterplay-grid">
<div class="counterplay-cell"><div class="counterplay-phase">Pre-Activation</div><ul class="counterplay-methods"><li>Punish 0.5s post-cutscene</li><li>Force bad timing</li><li>Read patterns</li></ul></div>
<div class="counterplay-cell"><div class="counterplay-phase">Six Eyes</div><ul class="counterplay-methods"><li>Stay >12 studs from Blue</li><li>Kite 60s</li><li>Count Purple CD</li><li>Focus fire for Domain end</li></ul></div>
<div class="counterplay-cell"><div class="counterplay-phase">Post-Ult</div><ul class="counterplay-methods"><li>Punish burnout</li><li>Capitalize recovery</li><li>Bait nuke chant</li></ul></div>
</div>
</section>

<!-- YUJI -->
<section class="char-section" id="yuji">
<div class="char-header"><h2 class="char-name">Yuji Itadori / Sukuna</h2><div class="char-title">The Vessel & The King</div><div class="char-meta"><span>Rushdown / Dual-Form</span><span>A-Tier</span></div><p class="tagline">Timing combos > Feint mixups > Earned Sukuna awakening.</p></div>
<p class="description">Base Yuji = Divergent Fist timing + feint tech. Sukuna = earned unblockable catastrophe. Darkness through execution.</p>
<div class="section-label">3-Bar Integration</div>
<div class="bars-grid">
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">Ult</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li>Medium fill. Bonus BF chains.</li><li>R feint 6% (scale 6/8/10%).</li><li>G>Enchain mouth+2s cutscene>Sukuna 60s.</li><li>Post: Yuji, reset 0, 15s burnout.</li></ul></div>
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">Evasive</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li>Standard.</li><li>Cancels mistimed air slams.</li><li>Escape Sukuna unblockables.</li></ul></div>
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">R</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li>No CD instant.</li><li>6% Ult scaling.</li><li>5f recovery (8f same-move).</li><li>No feint Move 4.</li></ul></div>
</div>
<div class="section-label">Base Moves (Yuji)</div>
<table class="moves-table"><thead><tr><th class="input-col">In</th><th class="name-col">Name</th><th>Function</th><th class="block-col">Block</th><th class="notes-col">Details / VFX / SFX</th></tr></thead><tbody>
<tr><td class="input-col">1</td><td class="name-col">Cursed Strikes</td><td>Punch barrage</td><td class="block-col">Gnd Yes / Air No</td><td class="notes-col">Gnd 8 studs 10f safe. Air slam 10 stud cone 12f 10f lag. VFX: red eye glow + trail. SFX: rapid impacts. Feintable.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">2</td><td class="name-col">Crushing Blow</td><td>Two CE slams</td><td class="block-col">No</td><td class="notes-col">7 studs. 2nd launches 8 studs. 14f. 22f whiff. VFX: orange CE arcs. SFX: heavy impacts.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">3</td><td class="name-col">Divergent Fist</td><td>Timing mechanic</td><td class="block-col">No</td><td class="notes-col">Single=CE punch 12f. Double mistimed (8-10f)=2s stun. Double perfect (3-5f)=Black Flash. Back-hit 90deg=no CD chain x4 (+10% each), 4th always CDs. VFX: red flash + ripple. SFX: crisp crack.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">4</td><td class="name-col">Manji Kick</td><td>Counter</td><td class="block-col">N/A</td><td class="notes-col">No feint. 15-20f active. Not grabs/domains. Whiff 25f. Success launch 12 studs. VFX: guard aura + flash. SFX: hum > crack.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">R</td><td class="name-col">Feint</td><td>Cancel to neutral</td><td class="block-col">N/A</td><td class="notes-col">Instant. 6% scaling. 5f recovery. VFX: red flash. SFX: whoosh tell.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">Air1</td><td class="name-col">Downward Slam</td><td>Feet drop</td><td class="block-col">No</td><td class="notes-col">Feintable. 10f lag. VFX: red streak + dust. SFX: whoosh > thud.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">Air2</td><td class="name-col">Air Dash Slams</td><td>1s hover > 2 slams</td><td class="block-col">No</td><td class="notes-col">Feintable. 15f lag. VFX: horizontal trail. SFX: dash > double impact.</td></tr>
</tbody></table>
<div class="section-label">Ultimate: Sukuna</div>
<div class="ult-box">
<div class="ult-name">Sukuna Awakening</div>
<div class="ult-voiceline">Enchain.</div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Moves</div><ul class="ult-detail-text"><li><strong>Dismantle (1):</strong> Multi-slash. UNBLOCKABLE. 12 studs. No tracking. 8f. 16f whiff. VFX: invisible slashes + sparks. SFX: rapid slicing.</li><li><strong>Fuga (2):</strong> Fire arrow. UNBLOCKABLE. +30% in Shrine. Startup 1.5s: cancelable, vulnerable, self-dmg zone 8 studs 5DPS, last 0.3s armor. Active 0.8s: full i-frames. Recovery 0.5s. VFX: flaming arrow + heat haze. SFX: ignition > scream > detonation.</li><li><strong>Rush (3):</strong> Dash>uppercut>TP>downslam. UNBLOCKABLE. 0.2s telegraph. Uppercut whiff 25f. Downslam lag 15f. VFX: red trail + crater. SFX: whoosh > crack > zip > shatter.</li><li><strong>Shrine (4):</strong> 12s domain. Block -50%. Dash-out first 0.5s only. Ends early >30% HP. VFX: shrine backdrop + red slashes. SFX: chant > hum.</li><li><strong>Cleave (R):</strong> Neck grab + cuts. UNBLOCKABLE. 6 studs. 8% timer. 20f whiff. VFX: slash blur. SFX: grab > slicing.</li><li><strong>Air WCS (Air1):</strong> ~40% ground WCS dmg. Safe on block.</li></ul></div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">World Cutting Slash (1>3>2>R)</div><ul class="ult-detail-text"><li>0.3s between inputs. Move 1 off-CD required. 2/3 CD bypassed.</li><li>Ignores block. Massive true dmg. i-frames 0.8s. Costs 15% timer.</li><li>VFX: reality-splitting slash + screen shake. SFX: silence > tear > boom.</li></ul></div>
</div>
<div class="section-label">Balance Safeguards</div>
<ul class="safeguards-list"><li>Feint 6% scaling + 5f recovery.</li><li>BF 3-5f window. Chain cap 4.</li><li>Manji 15-20f / whiff 25f.</li><li>Air lag 10f/15f.</li><li>Dismantle no tracking.</li><li>Fuga startup interruptible.</li><li>Rush telegraph + whiff punish.</li><li>Shrine dash-out 0.5s.</li><li>Cleave 6 studs + 8% timer.</li><li>WCS exact sequence + 15% timer.</li><li>Burnout 15s.</li></ul>
<div class="section-label">Counterplay</div>
<div class="counterplay-grid">
<div class="counterplay-cell"><div class="counterplay-phase">Yuji</div><ul class="counterplay-methods"><li>Block Gnd 1</li><li>Space 6 studs</li><li>Turn vs back-hit</li><li>Punish Manji whiff</li><li>Read feint tells</li></ul></div>
<div class="counterplay-cell"><div class="counterplay-phase">Sukuna</div><ul class="counterplay-methods"><li>Space 12 studs Dismantle</li><li>Interrupt Fuga 1.2s</li><li>Dash Shrine 0.5s</li><li>Punish Rush whiff</li><li>Survive 60s</li></ul></div>
</div>

<div class="ult-box">
<div class="ult-name">Jacob's Ladder</div>
<div class="ult-voiceline">Oh Here We Go.</div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Trigger</div><div class="ult-detail-text">4 landed katana swings > "USE AGAIN" > press again > crosshair aim target.</div></div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Sequence (~6.6s)</div><ul class="ult-detail-text"><li>0.3s aim confirm (golden reticle).</li><li>1s Yuta raises hand, moves down. "Oh Here We Go."</li><li>0.8s DOMAIN BARRIER SHATTERS (domain ends). Golden swirling sky rings manifest.</li><li>1.5s GOLDEN VERY SHINY pillar descends from rings. Target STUNNED.</li><li>2s target avatar COMPLETE BLACK ascending slowly into light.</li><li>1s aftermath. Yuta 15f recovery.</li></ul></div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Damage</div><div class="ult-detail-text">80 FLAT true damage. Constant. No scaling. Near-instant kill.</div></div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Cost</div><div class="ult-detail-text">Domain shatters = sacrifice remaining domain duration. Yuta i-frames phases 2-5.</div></div>
</div>
<div class="section-label">Balance Safeguards</div>
<ul class="safeguards-list"><li>8 slot cap FIFO.</li><li>Copies lost on death.</li><li>Beam lock after 1s.</li><li>Katana miss consumed.</li><li>Ladder needs 4 LANDED.</li><li>Domain shatters as cost.</li><li>Shared 2m30s timer.</li><li>Rika 1 blockable.</li><li>Elbow rush telegraph.</li><li>Ripple close-range risk.</li></ul>
<div class="section-label">Counterplay</div>
<div class="counterplay-grid">
<div class="counterplay-cell"><div class="counterplay-phase">Base</div><ul class="counterplay-methods"><li>Block Rika downslam (deny copy)</li><li>Sidestep elbow rush</li><li>Punish beam cancel window</li></ul></div>
<div class="counterplay-cell"><div class="counterplay-phase">Domain</div><ul class="counterplay-methods"><li>Dash katana swings</li><li>Body-block targeting</li><li>Burst during collection</li></ul></div>
<div class="counterplay-cell"><div class="counterplay-phase">Ladder</div><ul class="counterplay-methods"><li>See rings form = know it's coming</li><li>Stunned, accept fate</li></ul></div>
</div>
</section>

<!-- NANAMI -->
<section class="char-section" id="nanami">
<div class="char-header"><h2 class="char-name">Nanami Kento</h2><div class="char-title">The Overtime Sorcerer</div><div class="char-meta"><span>Ratio Technician / Punisher</span><span>S-Tier</span></div><p class="tagline">Hidden ratio > Forced weak points > Overtime devastation.</p></div>
<p class="description">Two-step ratio: R starts moving circle (faster at lower HP), R again stops it exactly on red line = 10s locked window. Confirm with move or 4 M1 (4th=BF). Overtime = tie off, no R CD, 500-dmg cinematic.</p>
<div class="section-label">3-Bar Integration</div>
<div class="bars-grid">
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">Ult</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li>Medium fill. Bonus ratio hits + BF.</li><li>G: 2.5s cutscene (remove tie, tie hand, "How unfortunate. I'm going to have to work overtime.") 2m timer.</li><li>Post: reset 0, 15s burnout.</li></ul></div>
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">Evasive</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li>Standard.</li><li>Escape Collapse + Interrogate chase.</li></ul></div>
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">R</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li>4s CD. NO CD if ratio locked AND follow-up move lands.</li><li>Overtime: NO CD regardless.</li><li>Marker INVISIBLE to enemy.</li><li>Base: 0.5s black sparks near stomach tell. Disabled in ult.</li></ul></div>
</div>
<div class="section-label">Ratio Flow</div>
<div class="ult-box">
<div class="ult-name">Ratio Technique (Two-Step)</div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Step 1</div><div class="ult-detail-text">Aim crosshair > R. Diagram appears (Nanami only): red line (random weak point) + white lines + moving circle. Circle speed scales with LOWER enemy HP.</div></div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Step 2</div><div class="ult-detail-text">R again to STOP circle. Exactly on red line = LOCKED 10s. Miss = disappears, 4s CD. No 2nd press = expires.</div></div>
<div class="ult-detail"><div class="ult-detail-label">Step 3</div><div class="ult-detail-text">Land any move OR 4 M1 within 10s. Confirm = R CD reset + ratio bonus + stomach sparks (base). 4th M1 after confirm = ALWAYS Black Flash. Fail = diagram gone, CD applies.</div></div>
</div>

<tr><td class="input-col">3</td><td class="name-col">Blunt Cut</td><td>HOLD 3 stages. S2/S3 break block. S3 auto</td><td class="block-col">S1 Yes / S2-3 No</td><td class="notes-col">Ratio = more dmg + heavy blood mouth VFX. VFX: blunt blade glow. SFX: building hum > crack.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">4</td><td class="name-col">Stab</td><td>Blade stab. Stun</td><td class="block-col">No</td><td class="notes-col">Ratio = DISLOCATES random limb (arm/leg/torso) 8s. Disabled moves per limb. VFX: stab + joint pop. SFX: pierce > crack.</td></tr>

<!-- GOJO -->
<section class="char-section" id="gojo">
<div class="char-header"><h2 class="char-name">Gojo Satoru</h2><div class="char-title">The Honored One</div><div class="char-meta"><span>Zoner / Combo Technician</span><span>S-Tier</span></div><p class="tagline">Precision teleport combos > Earned 60s unblockable window > Tactical domain/nuke decisions.</p></div>
<p class="description">Controlled omnipotence. Base kit = teleport combos managing Ult as resource. Six Eyes = 60s unblockable force. Mastery = WHEN to deploy Domain, Nuke, Boomerang.</p>
<div class="section-label">3-Bar Integration</div>
<div class="bars-grid">
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">Ult</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li><strong>Fill:</strong> Slow. Bonus on BF/R-combos.</li><li><strong>Cost:</strong> R 5-10%, recoup 3-7% on hit. Net 2-3%.</li><li><strong>Activate:</strong> G>2s cutscene+i-frames>60s timer. Pauses if stunned.</li><li><strong>Post:</strong> Reset 0. 15s burnout +5% R.</li></ul></div>
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">Evasive</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li>Standard gain.</li><li>Cancels BASE Blue pull.</li><li>USELESS vs Six Eyes Max Blue.</li><li>Disabled inside Unlimited Void.</li></ul></div>
<div class="bar-cell"><div class="bar-label">R</div><ul class="bar-detail"><li>8s CD. Glass break SFX.</li><li>Base 5-10% Ult cost.</li><li>Six Eyes: free TP, chant, 0.2 feint.</li><li>Burnout +5% for 15s.</li></ul></div>
</div>
<div class="section-label">Base Moves</div>
<table class="moves-table"><thead><tr><th class="input-col">In</th><th class="name-col">Name</th><th>Function</th><th class="block-col">Block</th><th class="notes-col">Details / VFX / SFX</th></tr></thead><tbody>

<tr><td class="input-col">3</td><td class="name-col">Rapid Punches</td><td>6-hit barrage</td><td class="block-col">No</td><td class="notes-col">6-8 studs. NO tracking. Last hit endlag if blocked. 22f whiff. VFX: blue blur trails. SFX: rapid impacts + distinct final.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">4</td><td class="name-col">Twofold Kick</td><td>2-hit kick, launch</td><td class="block-col">No</td><td class="notes-col">Launches ~15 studs 45deg. 14f startup. 24f whiff. VFX: blue arcs + upward burst. SFX: heavy kicks + whoosh.</td></tr>
<tr><td class="input-col">R</td><td class="name-col">Teleport</td><td>Instant reposition</td><td class="block-col">N/A</td><td class="notes-col">5-10% Ult. Recoup on combo. SFX: glass break (universal tell).</td></tr>
